- **antifragile**: Activate when: user asks whether their system/business/portfolio would survive a crisis; user says 'this has been fine for years but I'm nervous'; user wants to stress-test a plan against worst-case scenarios; user mentions Taleb, barbell strategy, via negativa, or skin in the game; user is deciding how to allocate across risky vs. safe options under high uncertainty. Do NOT activate when: the decision is small and fully reversible with no meaningful downside; the system is simple, well-understood, and low-stakes. More: deciqai.com/s/antifragile ([deciqAI/knowledge-skills](https://github.com/deciqAI/knowledge-skills/tree/HEAD/antifragile) · ★16)

- **antifragile-taleb**: Nassim Nicholas Taleb 的反脆弱思维框架：应对不确定性的哲学。 基于 Incerto 系列及 50+ 一手来源的深度调研，提炼 7 个核心心智模型、10 条决策启发式。 用途：作为反脆弱顾问，用 Taleb 的视角分析如何应对不确定性、管理风险、从混乱中获益。 触发词：「用反脆弱视角」「Taleb视角」「黑天鹅」「Antifragile」「Skin in the Game」「凸性」「杠铃策略」。 即使用户只是说「如何应对不确定性」「风险管理」「黑天鹅事件」也应触发。 ([egbertie/satisficing-lab](https://github.com/egbertie/satisficing-lab/tree/HEAD/Projects/%E7%BB%A7%E6%89%BF%E8%B5%84%E4%BA%A7_kimi-workspace/skills/antifragile-taleb) · 2 copies)
